### Task 17: Design review against the approved comp

**Model: sonnet**

**Files:** none are edited unless the review finds a defect; then `ui/styles.css`, `ui/Ambient.tsx`, `ui/Settings.tsx` or `ui/App.tsx`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the pictures of Task 15 and the approved comp `E:\Documentos\ProjetosIA\faber-notas\design-final\` (`index.html`, `tokens.json`, `notes.md`, and `telas-<look>\s1.png`, `s5.png`; `<look>` is `sala`, `oficina`, `bancada`, `manha`, `alto`, `cobalto`).
- Produces: a list of material defects, fixed, or the statement that there are none.

**Proof commands that must pass at the end:** `cargo t --suite screen` (as before).

This task is the human-level check that no test can make: whether the six looks look like the approved design. The review uses the installed `impeccable` skill as the judge.

- [ ] **Step 1: Take fresh pictures**

Run: `cargo t --suite screen`
Expected: `18 passed`; `target/faber-test/shots/` holds `s1-sala.png`, `s1-oficina.png`, `s1-bancada.png`, `s1-manha.png`, `s1-alto.png`, `s1-cobalto.png` (1440x900), the same with `-1280`, `settings-workbench.png` and `settings-save-failed.png`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the review**

Invoke the `impeccable` skill, command `critique`, with these instructions. Do not use any command that builds or redesigns (`shape`, `craft`, `bolder`, `overdrive`, `colorize`, `layout`, `typeset`); the brief wins, so the approved comp is the brief and this review is a judgement of fidelity and defects.

1. Look at each picture of Step 1 with the Read tool, and open the matching comp picture next to it (`telas-<look>\s1.png`; for Settings, `telas-sala\s5.png` and `telas-bancada\s5.png`, for tokens only).
2. Judge colour, type (family, weight, width, size, tracking), radius, borders, shadow, spacing rhythm and contrast, and the three effects (Dark Room and Cobalt: the projector beam with dust; Workshop: the loom at the bottom right; Workbench: the flow line at the top right). Also judge plain defects: clipped or overlapping text, an effect over words the person reads, a control without a visible selected state, anything outside the stage, and obvious rendering errors.
3. Do not report these differences, which belong to later pieces and which the spec forbids building here: the lockup logo (B4); the top bar's status chips (B4); the sidebar's search, project list and history (B5); the personal greeting, suggestions and "Continue where you left off" (B5b); Settings' final layout (B8); the loom and the flow line being hidden in narrow windows (Deviation 8); the English interface text.
4. Take pictures that are not mid-animation: the controls fade for 140 ms and the alert arrives over 400 ms. The scenarios already wait for that; a picture that still shows two selected options in one group, or a faint alert, is a bad picture, not a defect.

Expected: either a list of material defects, most serious first, each with the picture, what is wrong and the file that owns it, or "no material defects". When the plan was written, this review found four things, now fixed and already in the code of this plan: the flow line over the Workbench heading in a 1280 px window, the serif navigation font on the new-chat button in Workshop, and two pictures taken mid-animation. A second run after the fixes listed only the two items of Deviation 17: accepted, change nothing for them.

- [ ] **Step 3: Fix what is material, at most twice**

Fix each defect in the file that owns it, with the smallest change that makes the picture match the comp. After each round run `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck` and `cargo t --suite screen`, and look at the pictures again. Stop after two rounds: a defect that is still there after two is reported to the maintainer instead of being worked around, and it is not a reason to weaken a scenario.

If a fix changes `ui/styles.css`, `ui/Ambient.tsx`, `ui/Settings.tsx` or `ui/App.tsx`, Task 18's manifest no longer matches for that file; that is expected, and the maintainer is told which file changed and why.

projector beam, the loom and the flow line of the new conversation, and the
